000721c8 const int kFillBW = 0;const int kFillAA = 1;const int kInverseFillBW = 2;const int kInverseFillAA = 3;
0007222f         uniform int edgeType;  // GrClipEdgeType, specialized
0007226d         uniform float4 rectUniform;
00072292         half4 main(float2 xy, half4 inColor) {
000722c1             half coverage;
000722dc             if (edgeType == kFillBW || edgeType == kInverseFillBW) {
00072321                 // non-AA
0007233b                 coverage = all(greaterThan(float4(sk_FragCoord.xy, rectUniform.zw),
0007238f                                            float4(rectUniform.xy, sk_FragCoord.xy))) ? 1 : 0;
000723ed             } else {
00072402                 // compute coverage relative to left and right edges, add, then subtract 1 to
00072460                 // account for double counting. And similar for top/bottom.
000724ac                 half4 dists4 = clamp(half4(1, 1, -1, -1) *
000724e7                                      half4(sk_FragCoord.xyxy - rectUniform), 0, 1);
0007253b                 half2 dists2 = dists4.xy + dists4.zw - 1;
00072575                 coverage = dists2.x * dists2.y;
000725a5             }
000725b4             if (edgeType == kInverseFillBW || edgeType == kInverseFillAA) {
00072600                 coverage = 1.0 - coverage;
0007262b             }
0007263a             return inColor * coverage;
00072661         }
0007266b     
00072671         // Effect that is a LUT for integral of normal distribution. The value at x:[0,6*sigma] is
000726d4         // the integral from -inf to (3*sigma - x). I.e. x is mapped from [0, 6*sigma] to
0007272e         // [3*sigma to -3*sigma]. The flip saves a reversal in the shader.
00072779         uniform shader integral;
0007279b         uniform float4 rect;
000727b8         uniform int isFast;  // specialized
000727e5         half4 main(float2 pos, half4 inColor) {
00072815             half xCoverage, yCoverage;
0007283c             if (bool(isFast)) {
0007285c                 // Get the smaller of the signed distance from the frag coord to the left and right
000728c0                 // edges and similar for y.
000728ec                 // The integral texture goes "backwards" (from 3*sigma to -3*sigma), So, the below
0007294f                 // computations align the left edge of the integral texture with the inset rect's
000729b1                 // edge extending outward 6 * sigma from the inset rect.
000729fa                 half2 xy = max(half2(rect.LT - pos), half2(pos - rect.RB));
00072a46                 xCoverage = integral.eval(half2(xy.x, 0.5)).a;
00072a85                 yCoverage = integral.eval(half2(xy.y, 0.5)).a;
00072ac4             } else {
00072ad9                 // We just consider just the x direction here. In practice we compute x and y
00072b37                 // separately and multiply them together.
00072b71                 // We define our coord system so that the point at which we're evaluating a kernel
00072bd4                 // defined by the normal distribution (K) at 0. In this coord system let L be left
00072c37                 // edge and R be the right edge of the rectangle.
00072c79                 // We can calculate C by integrating K with the half infinite ranges outside the
00072cda                 // L to R range and subtracting from 1:
00072d12                 //   C = 1 - <integral of K from from -inf to  L> - <integral of K from R to inf>
00072d74                 // K is symmetric about x=0 so:
00072da4                 //   C = 1 - <integral of K from from -inf to  L> - <integral of K from -inf to -R>
00072e09                 // The integral texture goes "backwards" (from 3*sigma to -3*sigma) which is
00072e66                 // factored in to the below calculations.
00072ea0                 // Also, our rect uniform was pre-inset by 3 sigma from the actual rect being
00072efe                 // blurred, also factored in.
00072f2c                 half4 rect = half4(half2(rect.LT - pos), half2(pos - rect.RB));
00072f7c                 xCoverage = 1 - integral.eval(half2(rect.L, 0.5)).a
00072fc0                               - integral.eval(half2(rect.R, 0.5)).a;
00073005                 yCoverage = 1 - integral.eval(half2(rect.T, 0.5)).a
00073049                               - integral.eval(half2(rect.B, 0.5)).a;
0007308e             }
0007309c             return inColor * xCoverage * yCoverage;
000730d0         }
000730da     
000730df const int kFillBW = 0;const int kFillAA = 1;const int kInverseFillBW = 2;const int kInverseFillAA = 3;
00073146         uniform int edgeType;      // GrClipEdgeType, specialized
00073188         uniform int medPrecision;  // !sk_Caps.floatIs32Bits, specialized
000731d3         uniform float4 ellipse;
000731f3         uniform float2 scale;    // only for medPrecision
0007322e         half4 main(float2 xy, half4 inColor) {
0007325d             // d is the offset to the ellipse center
00073292             float2 d = sk_FragCoord.xy - ellipse.xy;
000732c7             // If we're on a device with a "real" mediump then we'll do the distance computation in
0007332b             // a space that is normalized by the larger radius or 128, whichever is smaller. The
0007338c             // scale uniform will be scale, 1/scale. The inverse squared radii uniform values are
000733ee             // already in this normalized space. The center is not.
00073432             if (bool(medPrecision)) {
00073458                 d *= scale.y;
00073476             }
00073484             float2 Z = d * ellipse.zw;
000734ab             // implicit is the evaluation of (x/rx)^2 + (y/ry)^2 - 1.
000734f1             float implicit = dot(Z, d) - 1;
0007351d             // grad_dot is the squared length of the gradient of the implicit.
0007356c             float grad_dot = 4 * dot(Z, Z);
00073598             // Avoid calling inversesqrt on zero.
000735ca             if (bool(medPrecision)) {
000735f0                 grad_dot = max(grad_dot, 6.1036e-5);
00073625             } else {
0007363a                 grad_dot = max(grad_dot, 1.1755e-38);
00073670             }
0007367e             float approx_dist = implicit * inversesqrt(grad_dot);
000736c0             if (bool(medPrecision)) {
000736e6                 approx_dist *= scale.x;
0007370e             }
0007371d             half alpha;
00073735             if (edgeType == kFillBW) {
0007375c                 alpha = approx_dist > 0.0 ? 0.0 : 1.0;
00073793             } else if (edgeType == kFillAA) {
000737c1                 alpha = saturate(0.5 - half(approx_dist));
000737fc             } else if (edgeType == kInverseFillBW) {
00073831                 alpha = approx_dist > 0.0 ? 1.0 : 0.0;
00073868             } else {  // edgeType == kInverseFillAA
0007389c                 alpha = saturate(0.5 + half(approx_dist));
000738d7             }
000738e5             return inColor * alpha;
00073909         }
00073913     
00073919         uniform colorFilter fp;
00073939         half4 main(half4 inColor) {
0007395d             return fp.eval(inColor.rgb1) * inColor.a;
00073993         }
0007399d     
000739a3         uniform shader ninePatchFP;
000739c8         uniform half cornerRadius;
000739eb         uniform float4 proxyRect;
00073a0d         uniform half blurRadius;
00073a2f         half4 main(float2 xy, half4 inColor) {
00073a5e             // Warp the fragment position to the appropriate part of the 9-patch blur texture by
00073abf             // snipping out the middle section of the proxy rect.
00073b01             float2 translatedFragPosFloat = sk_FragCoord.xy - proxyRect.LT;
00073b4d             float2 proxyCenter = (proxyRect.RB - proxyRect.LT) * 0.5;
00073b93             half edgeSize = 2.0 * blurRadius + cornerRadius + 0.5;
00073bd7             // Position the fragment so that (0, 0) marks the center of the proxy rectangle.
00073c34             // Negative coordinates are on the left/top side and positive numbers are on the
00073c91             // right/bottom.
00073cae             translatedFragPosFloat -= proxyCenter;
00073ce2             // Temporarily strip off the fragment's sign. x/y are now strictly increasing as we
00073d42             // move away from the center.
00073d6c             half2 fragDirection = half2(sign(translatedFragPosFloat));
00073db3             translatedFragPosFloat = abs(translatedFragPosFloat);
00073df6             // Our goal is to snip out the "middle section" of the proxy rect (everything but the
00073e58             // edge). We've repositioned our fragment position so that (0, 0) is the centerpoint
00073eb9             // and x/y are always positive, so we can subtract here and interpret negative results
00073f1c             // as being within the middle section.
00073f4f             half2 translatedFragPosHalf = half2(translatedFragPosFloat - (proxyCenter - edgeSize));
00073fb4             // Remove the middle section by clamping to zero.
00073ff2             translatedFragPosHalf = max(translatedFragPosHalf, 0);
00074036             // Reapply the fragment's sign, so that negative coordinates once again mean left/top
00074098             // side and positive means bottom/right side.
000740d2             translatedFragPosHalf *= fragDirection;
00074107             // Offset the fragment so that (0, 0) marks the upper-left again, instead of the center
0007416b             // point.
00074181             translatedFragPosHalf += half2(edgeSize);
000741b8             half2 proxyDims = half2(2.0 * edgeSize);
000741ed             half2 texCoord = translatedFragPosHalf / proxyDims;
0007422e             return inColor * ninePatchFP.eval(texCoord).a;
00074269         }
00074273     
00074279         uniform shader blurProfile;
0007429d         uniform half4 circleData;
000742c0         half4 main(float2 xy, half4 inColor) {
000742ef             // We just want to compute "(length(vec) - circleData.z + 0.5) * circleData.w" but need
00074353             // to rearrange to avoid passing large values to length() that would overflow.
000743ae             half2 vec = half2((sk_FragCoord.xy - circleData.xy) * circleData.w);
000743ff             half dist = length(vec) + (0.5 - circleData.z) * circleData.w;
0007444a             return inColor * blurProfile.eval(half2(dist, 0.5)).a;
0007448d         }
00074497     
0007449d         uniform colorFilter fp;  // Declared as colorFilter so we can pass a color
000744f0         half4 main(half4 src, half4 dst) {
0007451b             return fp.eval(dst);
0007453c         }
00074546     
0007454c         half4 main(half4 inColor) {
00074570             half factor = 1 - inColor.a;
00074599             factor = exp(-factor * factor * 4) - 0.018;
000745d1             return half4(factor);
000745f3         }
000745fd     
00074603         uniform colorFilter fp;  // Declared as colorFilter so we can pass a color
00074656         uniform half4 color;
00074673         half4 main(half4 inColor) {
00074697             return fp.eval(color);
000746ba         }
000746c4     
000746ca         half4 main(half4 inColor) {
000746ee             return saturate(inColor);
00074714         }
0007471e     
00074724         uniform float4 scale[2];
00074745         uniform float4 bias[2];
00074765         uniform half threshold;
00074786         half4 main(float2 coord) {
000747a9             half t = half(coord.x);
000747ce             float4 s, b;
000747e7             if (t < threshold) {
00074808                 s = scale[0];
00074826                 b = bias[0];
00074843             } else {
00074858                 s = scale[1];
00074876                 b = bias[1];
00074893             }
000748a2             return half4(t * s + b);
000748c7         }
000748d1     
000748d7         uniform half range;
000748f3         uniform shader table;
00074911         half4 main(float2 xy, half4 color) {
0007493e             half value = table.eval(sk_FragCoord.xy).a - 0.5; // undo the bias in the table
0007499a             // For each color channel, add the random offset to the channel value and then clamp
000749fb             // between 0 and alpha to keep the color premultiplied.
00074a3f             return half4(clamp(color.rgb + value * range, 0.0, color.a), color.a);
00074a92         }
00074a9c     
00074aa2         half4 main(half4 c) {
00074ac0             half4 p = (c.g < c.b) ? half4(c.bg, -1,  2/3.0)
00074afc                                   : half4(c.gb,  0, -1/3.0);
00074b39             half4 q = (c.r < p.x) ? half4(p.x, c.r, p.yw)
00074b73                                   : half4(c.r, p.x, p.yz);
00074baf             // q.x  -> max channel value
00074bd8             // q.yz -> 2nd/3rd channel values (unsorted)
00074c11             // q.w  -> bias value dependent on max channel selection
00074c57             half eps = 0.0001;
00074c76             half pmV = q.x;
00074c92             half pmC = pmV - min(q.y, q.z);
00074cbe             half pmL = pmV - pmC * 0.5;
00074ce6             half   H = abs(q.w + (q.y - q.z) / (pmC * 6 + eps));
00074d27             half   S = pmC / (c.a + eps - abs(pmL * 2 - c.a));
00074d66             half   L = pmL / (c.a + eps);
00074d91             return half4(H, S, L, c.a);
00074db9         }
00074dc3     
00074dc9         uniform half4 start;
00074de6         uniform half4 end;
00074e01         half4 main(float2 coord) {
00074e24             // Clamping and/or wrapping was already handled by the parent shader so the output
00074e83             // color is a simple lerp.
00074eaa             return mix(start, end, half(coord.x));
00074edd         }
00074ee7     
00074eed         uniform shader src;
00074f09         uniform float4 boundsUniform;
00074f2f         uniform float  xInvZoom;
00074f50         uniform float  yInvZoom;
00074f71         uniform float  xInvInset;
00074f93         uniform float  yInvInset;
00074fb5         uniform half2  offset;
00074fd5         half4 main(float2 coord) {
00074ff8             float2 zoom_coord = offset + coord * float2(xInvZoom, yInvZoom);
00075045             float2 delta = (coord - boundsUniform.xy) * boundsUniform.zw;
0007508f             delta = min(delta, float2(1.0) - delta);
000750c4             delta *= float2(xInvInset, yInvInset);
000750f8             float weight = 0.0;
00075118             if (delta.s < 2.0 && delta.t < 2.0) {
0007514a                 delta = float2(2.0) - delta;
00075177                 float dist = length(delta);
000751a3                 dist = max(2.0 - dist, 0.0);
000751d0                 weight = min(dist * dist, 1.0);
00075200             } else {
00075215                 float2 delta_squared = delta * delta;
0007524b                 weight = min(min(delta_squared.x, delta_squared.y), 1.0);
00075295             }
000752a4             return src.eval(mix(coord, zoom_coord, weight));
000752e1         }
